Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（僅供參考）


ANDROID SMART E-PAYMENT SOLUTIONS
安卓智能電子支付解決方案

General features:   Powered by Android operating system and PCI PTS certified. Compact, portable, equipped with a large
                    high resolution touch screen and inbuilt camera. Support various kinds of payment options, including
                    bank card payments, Near Field Communication (“NFC”) contactless and QR code payments.
一般特點：               以安卓作業系統操作，並獲得PCI PTS認証。外型小巧，方便手持，具有特大高清觸控屏及內置攝像
                    頭。支援各種支付方式，包括銀行卡支付、NFC非觸式及二維碼支付等。

Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（僅供參考）


UNATTENDED SOLUTIONS
自助服務解決方案

General features:   Ideal for self-service sales points such as railway stations, petrol stations, parking lots and kiosks.
                    Products are certified with PCI PTS and have met required standards for anti-vandalism and
                    waterproofing. Support various kinds of payment options and connectivity options include 3G, 4G, WiFi
                    and Bluetooth.
一般特點：               適用於火車站、加油站、停車場、售貨亭等自助銷售場景。產品獲得PCI PTS認證，符合防爆和防水標
                    準。可支援各種支付方式及3G、4G、WiFi和藍牙多個連接選項。




 IM30                                                                SK700


CLASSIC E-PAYMENT SOLUTIONS
傳統電子支付解決方案

General Features:   Traditional Linux portfolio with PCI PTS certified, ranging from the D-Series, Q-Series, S-Series, and more.
                    Support bank card payments, NFC contactless payments, and more.
一般特點：               具備PCI PTS認證的傳統Linux產品，涵蓋D系列、Q系列、 S系列等。支援銀行卡支付、NFC非接觸式支
                    付等。

Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（僅供參考）


COMMERCIAL EPOS SOLUTIONS
商業EPOS解決方案

General Features:    Feature a suite of software and hardware for business Internet of Things, such as the handheld scanner,
                     the external printer, as well as a commercial Kitchen Display System (“KDS”). Powered by Android, these
                     devices seamlessly interconnect and collaborate between in-store POS devices.
一般特點：                涵濭－系列商業物聯網的軟硬件配套，包含手提掃描器、外置式打印機、商用廚房屏顯系統等。以安
                     卓作為驅動，使店內POS設備之間實現無縫互連和協同。

Market Analysis and Industry Trends                                           市場分析及行業趨勢
In 2023, the recovery of global economy still encountered substantial         二 零 二 三 年，全 球 經 濟 復 甦 仍 面 對 著 重 大 挑
challenges. High inflation, as well as exchange rate fluctuations, remain     戰。通 脹 高 企 及 匯 率 波 動 依 然 是 眾 多 國 際 客 戶
as primary concern for many global customers, leading to increased            的 主 要 憂 慮，因 此 支 付 終 端 佈 署 的 步 伐 更 為 審
prudence in payment terminal deployment, which in turn have an                慎，對本集團的收入帶來影響。
impact on the Group’s revenue.

PAX adeptly responded to market dynamics through the implementation           百 富 以 靈 活 的 調 整 策 略 應 對 市 場 變 化，進 一 步
of flexible strategies that further focus on high margin markets,             聚 焦 高 利 潤 的 市 場，並 取 得 良 好 進 展。受 惠 於
resulting in positive progress. Thanks to our diversified revenue structure   更 多 元 化 的 四 大 國 際 地 區 收 入 結 構，加 上 有 效
across four international regions, as well as cost reduction resulting from   的 成 本 控 制 策 略，以 及 人 民 幣 貶 值 令 成 本 相 應

not expressly permitted measures under GAAP in Hong Kong and may not be          原 則 所 明 確 認 可 的 指 標，故 未 必 可 與 其 他 公 司 的 同
comparable to similarly titled measures for other companies. Accordingly, such   類 指 標 作 比 較，因 此，該 等 非 公 認 會 計 原 則 指 標 不
non-GAAP measures should not be considered as an alternative to operating        應 視 作 經 營 收 入（作 為 本 集 團 業 務 表 現 指 標）的 替 補
income as an indicator of the operating performance of the Group or as an        或 經 營 活 動 現 金 流 量（作 為 衡 量 流 動 資 金）的 替 補。

The use of non-GAAP measures is provided solely to enhance the overall           財 務 表 現 的 整 體 理 解，此 外 由 於 本 集 團 以 往 曾 向 投

ENVIRONMENTAL POLICIES AND PERFORMANCE                                          環境政策及表現
The Group values the importance of protecting the environment in the            本 集 團 在 營 運 過 程 中 重 視 環 境 保 護 的 重 要 性。
process of operation. The Group’s major wastes comprise of terminal             本集團的廢棄物主要包括客戶使用及棄置的
machines and tools used and disposed by the customers. To mitigate              終 端 機 具。回 收 的 報 廢 機 具 會 交 由 政 府 認 可 的
the adverse impact on the environment, waste machines and tools                 回 收 公 司 進 行 電 子 垃 圾 回 收 和 分 解，以 減 低 對
are transferred to government-recognised recycling companies for                環 境 的 影 響。我 們 近 年 亦 加 強 環 保 辦 公 的 理 念
undergoing electronic waste recycling and decomposition. In recent              包 括 提 倡 無 紙 化、使 用 節 能 電 器 如LED光 管 及
years, we have also strengthened the green office concept by promoting          感 應 水 龍 頭、廢 物 分 類 及 回 收 和 鼓 勵 使 用 通 過
paperless office, use of energy-saving electrical appliances such as LED        視頻形式召開會議取代出差以減少能源使用
light pipes and inductive faucets, wastes classification and recycling, and     及碳足印。
encouraging video conferencing in place of physical business trips to

Hong Kong) (“SOGO”); “Supply of Services (Implied Terms)                        賣條例》」

Key scope                                                                       主要範圍
SOGO regulates the sales of goods by the Group in Hong Kong.                    《貨品售賣條例》規管本集團於香港的貨品售賣。
SOGO provides that, in a contract for the sale of goods by description,         《貨 品 售 賣 條 例 》規 定，憑 貨 品 說 明 售 貨 的 合
there is an implied condition that the goods shall correspond with the          約，均有貨品必須與貨品說明相符的隱含條件。
description. It also provides that where a seller sells goods in the course     它 亦 規 定，凡 賣 方 在 業 務 運 作 中 售 貨，有 一 項
of a business, there is an implied condition that the goods supplied            隱含的條件︰根據合約供應的貨品具可商售品
under the contract are of merchantable quality, except where an                 質，除非滿足例外條件。
exception condition is met.

SOSO regulates the supply of services by the Group in Hong Kong.                《服務提供（隱含條款）條例 》規管本集團於香港
SOSO provides that in a contract for the supply of service where the            的服務提供。    《服務提供（隱含條款）條例 》規定，
supplier is acting in the course of a business, there is an implied term        凡 提 供 人 是 在 業 務 過 程 中 行 事，則 在 有 關 的 服
that the supplier will carry out the service with reasonable care and           務提供合約中，即有一隱含條款，規定該人須以
skill. It also provides that under a contract for the supply of a service       合理程度的謹慎及技術作出服務。它亦規定，凡
where the supplier is acting in the course of a business, if the time           服務提供合約就提供人在業務過程中所提供的
for carrying out the service is not fixed by the contract or is not left        服務，沒有訂明作出服務的時間，或非以雙方的
to be determined by the course of dealing between the parties, there            交 易 過 程 來 決 定，則 在 該 合 約 中 即 有 一 隱 含 條
is an implied term that the supplier will carry out the service within a        款，規定提供人須在合理時間內作出該項服務。
reasonable time.

PENSION SCHEMES                                                             退休金計劃
The subsidiaries operating in Hong Kong are required to participate in      於香港營運的附屬公司須參加根據香港強制
a defined contribution retirement scheme (“MPF Scheme”) set up in           性公積金條例設立的定額供款退休計劃（「強積
accordance with the Hong Kong Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes              金 計 劃」），據 此，僱 員 和 僱 主 均 須 定 期 向 強 積
Ordinance, under which employers and employees are each required            金計劃作出供款（「強制性供款」），雙方的供款
to make regular mandatory contributions calculated at 5% of the             額 均 為 僱 員 有 關 入 息 的5%，並 受 限 於 法 定 最
employee’s relevant income (“Mandatory Contributions”) to the MPF           低 及 最 高 有 關 入 息 水 平。僱 員 也 可 選 擇 在 強 制
Scheme, subject to the minimum and maximum relevant income levels           性供款以外，作出額外的自願性供款。
as prescribed by law. Employees may also make voluntary contributions
additional to Mandatory Contributions.

Any Mandatory Contributions paid for and in respect of an employee          為僱員作出的強制性供款一經支付予強積金
are fully and immediately vested in the employee once paid to the           受 託 人，即 全 數 及 立 刻 歸 屬 於 該 僱 員。以 強 制

     (“E-payment Terminals”) products, provision of maintenance and              護 及 安 裝 及 支 付 解 決 方 案 服 務（統 稱「電
     installation and payment solution services (collectively, referred to       子支付終端解決方案業務」）          。
     as the “E-payment Terminals solutions business”).

     The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in                  本公司為一間在百慕達註冊成立的
     Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Clarendon House,           有 限 責 任 公 司，其 註 冊 辦 事 處 地 址 為

2    BASIS OF PREPARATION AND CHANGES IN                                     2   編制基準及會計政策變動
     ACCOUNTING POLICIES

     2.1 Basis of preparation                                                    2.1 編製基準
          The consolidated financial statements of the Company                          本公司的綜合財務報表已根據香港
          have been prepared in accordance with all applicable Hong                     會計師公會頒布的所有適用之香港
          Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) as issued                       財 務 報 告 準 則（「 香 港 財 務 報 告 準
          by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants                    則」）及 香 港《公 司 條 例 》（第622章）
          (“HKICPA”) and disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong                       的 披 露 規 定 編 製。除 按 公 平 值 計 量
          Companies Ordinance Cap. 622. The consolidated financial                      之金融資產及離職後褔利債務以公
          statements have been prepared under the historical cost                       平 值 計 量 外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 已 按 歷
          convention, except for financial assets measured at fair                      史成本法編製。
          value and post-employment benefit obligation, which are
          measured at fair values.

          The preparation of the consolidated financial statements in                   編製符合香港財務報告準則的綜合
          conformity with HKFRSs requires the use of certain critical                   財務報表須使用若干重要會計估
          accounting estimates. It also requires management to                          計，並 要 求 管 理 層 在 應 用 本 集 團 會
          exercise its judgment in the process of applying the Group’s                  計 政 策 時 作 出 判 斷。涉 及 更 高 程 度
          accounting policies. The areas involving a higher degree                      判斷或複雜性的領域或假設及估計
          of judgment or complexity, or areas where assumptions                         對綜合財務報表而言屬重大的領域
          and estimates are significant to the consolidated financial                   已於附註4披露。
          statements are disclosed in Note 4.

    2.2 Changes in accounting policies                                     2.2 會計政策變動
        (a) New and amended standards adopted by the                           (a) 本集團採納的新訂及經修訂
            Group                                                                  準則
            The Group has applied the following new and                            本集團已於二零二三年一月一
            amended standards for its annual reporting period                      日開始的年度報告期間應用下
            commencing 1 January 2023:                                             列新訂及經修訂準則：

            •    Disclosure of Accounting Policies – Amendments                    •   會 計 政 策 之 披 露－香 港
                 to HKAS 1 and HKFRS Practice Statement 2                              會計準則第 1 號及香港

     3.1 Financial risk factors                                                   3.1 財務風險因素
         The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:          本集團的活動使本集團承擔多種財
         market risk (including foreign exchange risk, price risk, cash             務 風 險：市 場 風 險（包 括 外 匯 風 險、

         markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse effects on                 針 對 金 融 市 場 的 不 可 預 測 性，力 求
         the Group’s financial performance.                                         將對本集團財務表現所造成的潛在
                                                                                    不利影響降至最低。

    3.2 Capital management                                                     3.2 資本管理
        The Group regards its shareholders’ equity as capital. The               本 集 團 將 其 股 東 權 益 視 為 資 本。本
        Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard                集團管理資本的目的為保障本集團
        the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order              能繼續以持續經營方式為股東提供
        to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other               回報以及為其他利益相關者帶來利
        stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to             益，並 且 維 持 最 佳 資 本 結 構 以 減 少
        reduce the cost of capital.                                              資本成本。

        In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the                為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集 團 可
        Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to                         調 整 向 股 東 支 付 股 息 的 金 額、將 資
        shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue new                  本 退 還 予 股 東、發 行 新 股 份、回 購
        shares, repurchase the Company’s shares or sell assets to                本公司股份或出售資產以減少債
        reduce debt.                                                             務。

        The Group monitors capital on the basis of gearing ratio.                本集團基於資本負債比率監控資
        This ratio is calculated as total debt divided by total capital.         本。該 比 率 按 債 務 總 額 除 以 資 本 總
        Total debt includes borrowings of the Group. Total capital is            額 計 算。債 務 總 額 包 括 本 集 團 的 借
        calculated as equity as shown in the consolidated balance                貸。資 本 總 額 為 列 於 綜 合 資 產 負 債
        sheet plus total debt.                                                   表的權益加上債務總額計算。

        The Group did not have any borrowings as at 31 December                  本集團於二零二三年十二月三十一
        2023 and the gearing ratio is not applicable (2022: same).               日並無借貸，資本負債比率並不適用
                                                                                 （二零二二年：同上）
                                                                                          。

      (b) Provision for inventories                                                (b)   存貨撥備
             Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable             存貨乃按成本及可變現淨值兩者之
             value. The cost of inventories is written down to net                       間 較 低 者 入 賬。倘 有 客 觀 證 據 顯 示
             realisable value when there is an objective evidence that                   存 貨 成 本 或 不 能 收 回，則 將 存 貨 成
             the cost of inventories may not be recoverable. The cost of                 本 撇 減 至 可 變 現 淨 值。倘 存 貨 過 時
             inventories may not be recoverable if those inventories are                 及 損 壞、全 部 或 部 分 陳 舊 或 售 價 下
             aged and damaged, if they have become wholly or partially                   跌，則 可 能 不 能 收 回 其 存 貨 成 本。
             obsolete, or if their selling prices have declined. The cost                倘 估 計 必 需 的 銷 售 成 本 增 加，亦 可
             of inventories may also not be recoverable if the estimated                 能導致其不能收回其存貨成本。
             costs necessary to make the sales have increased.

             The amount written off in the consolidated income                           於綜合收益表撇銷的金額為存貨的
             statement is the difference between the carrying value                      賬面值與其可變現淨值之間的差
             and net realisable value of the inventories. In determining                 額。於 釐 定 存 貨 價 值 是 否 可 以 被 收
             whether the inventories can be recoverable, significant                     回 時 需 作 出 重 大 判 斷。於 作 出 有 關
             judgment is required. In making this judgment, the Group                    判 斷 時，本 集 團 會 評 估 以 任 何 方 式
             evaluates, among other factors, the duration and extent by                  收 回 相 關 金 額 的 時 間 及 程 度（以 及
             all means to which the amount will be recovered.                            除此以外的其他因素）       。

      (c)    Income taxes and deferred taxation                                    (c)   所得稅及遞延稅項

             uncertain. Where the final tax outcome of these matters is                  等事項的最終稅務結果與最初記賬
             different from the amounts that were initially recorded, such               金 額 不 同 時，有 關 差 額 將 影 響 定 案

    (i) Revenue from sales of E-payment Terminals                             (i) 銷售電子支付終端產品所得收入
        products
           Revenue from sales of E-payment Terminals products are                    銷售電子支付終端產品所得收入
           recognised when control of the products has transferred,                  會於產品的控制權轉移時被確認，
           being when the products are delivered to the customers,                   即當產品交付予客戶，且客戶已接
           the customer has accepted the products and there is no                    納產品及無影響客戶接納產品的
           unfulfilled obligation that could affect the customer’s                   未履行責任。
           acceptance of the products.

           A receivable is recognised when the goods are delivered                   當產品交付時，即從此時間點開始
           as this is the point in time that the consideration is                    可 以 無 條 件（到 期 付 款 前 的 時 間 流
           unconditional because only the passage of time is required                逝 除 外）收 到 代 價 時，便 可 確 認 應
           before the payment is due.                                                收款項。

    (ii)   Revenue from provision of services                                 (ii)   提供服務所得收入
           Revenue from provision of services are recognised in the                  提供服務所得收入會於提供服務
           accounting period in which the services are rendered.                     的 會 計 期 間 內 確 認。維 修 及 安 裝
           Revenue from maintenance and installation and payment                     及支付解決方案服務所得收入乃
           solution services is recognised overtime based on the                     根據於報告期末實際已提供之服務
           actual service provided as at the end of the reporting                    （作 為 提 供 的 所 有 服 務 之 一 部 分）
           period as a proportion of the total services to be provided               隨時間予以確認，因為客戶會在同
           because the customer receives and consumes the benefits                   時間獲得及使用該利益。
           simultaneously.

    (iii) Contract liabilities                                                (iii) 合約負債

           the Group recognises the related revenue. The Group                       集團於綜合資產負債表中確認其

             calculation is done to determine the number of shares that              尚未行使購股權所附認購權的貨幣
             could have been acquired at fair value (determined as the               價 值 計 算，以 釐 定 可 按 公 平 值（定 為
             average market share price of the Company’s shares for the              本公司股份於期內的平均市價）購買
             period) based on the monetary value of the subscription                 的股份數目。按上述方式計算的股份
             rights attached to outstanding share options. The number of             數目與假設購股權獲行使時應已發

             shares that would have been issued assuming the exercise of

 14 PROPERTY, PLANT AND EQUIPMENT                                           14 物業、廠房及設備

       Material accounting policy                                             重大會計政策
       Depreciation of property, plant and equipment is calculated using      物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 的 折 舊 以 直 線 法 於 其
       the straight-line method to allocate cost to their residual values     估計可用年期內分配其成本至剩餘價
       over their estimated useful lives, as follows:                         值。所採用年率如下：

       Buildings                             2% - 10%                         樓宇              2% - 10%

       Leasehold improvements                20% or over lease terms,         租賃物業裝修          20%或按租賃年期
                                               whichever is shorter                            （以較短者為準）

       Office furniture and equipment        18% - 25%                        辦公室傢俱及設備        18% - 25%

       Plant and equipment                   9% - 33%                         廠房及設備           9% - 33%

       Motor vehicles                        18% - 25%                        汽車              18% - 25%

       No provision for depreciation is made on construction in progress      在相關資產完工並且可以用作擬定用途
       until such time as the relevant assets are completed and ready         前不會為在建工程計提折舊。
       for intended use.

       The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and         資產的剩餘價值及可用年期會於各報告
       adjusted if appropriate, at the end of each reporting period.          期末審閱及調整（如適用）
                                                                                         。

 15 RIGHT-OF-USE ASSETS AND LEASE LIABILITIES                                  15 使用權資產及租賃負債

      Material accounting policy                                                 重大會計政策
      (i) Right-of-use assets                                                    (i) 使用權資產
               Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the                   使用權資產一般於資產可用年期或
               shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on                 租 賃 期（以 較 短 者 為 準）內 按 直 線
               a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain                法予以折舊。倘本集團合理地確定
               to exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is                 會行使購買選擇權，則使用權資產
               depreciated over the underlying asset’s useful life.                     於相關資產的可使用年期內予以折
                                                                                        舊。

      (ii)     Lease liabilities                                                 (ii)   租賃負債
               Lease liabilities include the net present value of the                   租賃負債包括固定租賃付款的淨現
               fixed lease payments. Lease payments to be made under                    值。根據合理地確定會行使延續選
               reasonably certain extension options are also included in                擇權的租賃的租賃付款亦會計入其
               the measurement of the liability.                                        負債計量之內。

               The lease payments are discounted using the interest                     租賃付款採用租賃所隱含的利率予
               rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily               以貼現。倘無法輕易地釐定該利率
               determined, which is generally the case for leases in the                （為本集團租賃的一般情況），則使
               Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,                  用承租人的增量借貸利率，即個別
               being the rate that the individual lessee would have to                  承租人在類似經濟環境中按類似條
               pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                  款、抵押及條件借入獲得與使用權
               similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic            資產具有類似價值的資產所需的資
               environment with similar terms, security and conditions.                 金而必須支付的利率。

               To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group,                  為釐定增量借款利率，本集團在可
               where possible, uses recent third-party financing received               能情況下，使用個別承租人最近獲
               by the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                得的第三方融資為出發點並作出調
               reflect changes in financing conditions since third party                整，以反映自獲得第三方融資以來
               financing was received and makes adjustments specific to                 融資條件的變動，及進行特定於租
               the lease, e.g. term, country, currency and security.                    賃的相關調整，例如期限、國家、貨
                                                                                        幣及抵押。

15 RIGHT-OF-USE ASSETS AND LEASE LIABILITIES                               15 使用權資產及租賃負債（續）
   (continued)

   (c)   The Group’s leasing activities                                      (c)   本集團的租賃活動
         The cost of the land use rights represents the upfront                    土地使用權的成本指就使用土地的
         prepayments made for the rights to use the land for periods               權 利（為 期20至50年）而 支 付 之 預 付
         varying from 20 to 50 years.                                              款項。

         In addition to the land use rights, the Group leases various              除 土 地 使 用 權 外，本 集 團 租 賃 多 個
         office premises, factory and warehouses. Rental contracts                 辦 公 室 物 業、工 廠 及 倉 庫。辦 公 室
         of office premises, factory and warehouses are typically                  物 業、工 廠 及 倉 庫 的 租 賃 合 約 期 限
         made for fixed periods of 1 year to 10 years, but may have                通 常 固 定 為1至10年，惟 可 能 涵 蓋 如
         extension options as described in (d) below. Lease terms are              下 文(d)所 述 的 續 租 選 擇 權。租 賃 條
         negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range                款乃個別磋商及包含各種不同的條

      Material accounting policy                                                重大會計政策
      (i) Goodwill                                                              (i) 商譽
               Goodwill is not amortised but it is tested for impairment               商譽不會被攤銷，但會每年為其進
               annually, or more frequently if events or changes in                    行減值測試，倘有事件或情況變化
               circumstances indicate that it might be impaired, and is                顯示商譽可能出現減值情況，則會
               carried at cost less accumulated impairment losses.                     更 頻 密 地 進 行 測 試。以 及，商 譽 會
                                                                                       按成本減累計減值虧損列賬。

               Gains and losses on the disposal of an entity include the               出售實體的收益及虧損包括與被
               carrying amount of goodwill relating to the entity sold.                出售實體相關的商譽的賬面值。

               Goodwill is allocated to cash-generating units (“CGUs”)                 商譽會被分配至現金產生單位
               for the purpose of impairment testing. The allocation is                （「 現 金 產 生 單 位 」）以 作 減 值 測
               made to those cash-generating units or groups of cash-                  試。該等預期因業務合併而受惠的
               generating units that are expected to benefit from the                  現金產生單位或現金產生單位組
               business combination in which the goodwill arose. The                   別將獲分配從而所產生的商 譽。該
               units or groups of units are identified at the lowest level             等單位或單位組別被認定為就內
               at which goodwill is monitored for internal management                  部管理目的而對商譽進行監督的
               purposes.                                                               最低層級。

      (ii)     Brand names, contractual customer relationships                  (ii)   商標、合約客戶關係及其他
               and others
               Brand names, contractual customer relationships and                     於業務合併時所收購的商標、合約
               others acquired in a business combination are recognised                客戶關係及其他按照於收購日期
               at fair value at the acquisition date. They have a finite               的公平值確認。它們具有有限可使
               useful life and are subsequently carried at cost less                   用年期，及後會按照成本減累計攤
               accumulated amortisation and impairment losses.                         銷及減值虧損列賬。攤銷乃使用直
               Amortisation is calculated using the straight-line method               線 法 計 算，並 於 其 估 計 可 用 年 期5

17 FINANCIAL ASSETS MEASURED AT FAIR VALUE                                  17 按公平值計量之金融資產

    Material accounting policy                                                重大會計政策
    At initial recognition, the Group measures a financial asset at           於 初 始 確 認 時，如 非 按 公 平 值 計 入 損 益
    its fair value plus, in the case of a financial asset not at FVPL,        之 金 融 資 產，本 集 團 按 該 金 融 資 產 之 公
    transaction costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition       平值加直接歸屬於該金融資產收購之交
    of the financial asset. Transaction costs of financial assets carried     易 成 本 計 量。按 公 平 值 計 入 損 益 之 金 融
    at FVPL are expensed in the consolidated income statement.                資產之交易成本則於綜合收益表列作開
                                                                              支。

    Changes in the fair value of financial assets at FVPL are                按公平值計入損益之金融資產的公平值
    recognised in other gains/(loss), net in the consolidated income         變動會於綜合收益表確認為其他收益╱
    statement as applicable.                                                 （虧損）淨額（如適用）
                                                                                       。

    The Group subsequently measures all equity investments at fair            本集團後續按公平值計量所有權益投
    value. Where the Group’s management has elected to present                資。倘 本 集 團 管 理 層 選 擇 於 其 他 全 面 收
    fair value gains and losses on equity investments in other                益 列 報 權 益 投 資 之 公 平 值 收 益 及 虧 損，
    comprehensive income, there is no subsequent reclassification             於 終 止 確 認 投 資 後，概 不 會 後 續 重 新 分
    of fair value gains and losses to the profit or loss following            類 公 平 值 收 益 及 虧 損 至 損 益。當 本 集 團
    the derecognition of the investment. Dividends from such                  有 權 收 取 股 息 款 項 時，該 等 投 資 之 股 息
    investments continue to be recognised in the consolidated                 會繼續於綜合收益表內確認為其他收
    income statement as other income when the Group’s right to                入。按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收 益 之 權 益
    receive payments is established. Impairment losses (and reversal          投資之減值虧損（及減值虧損撥回）不會
    of impairment losses) on equity investments measured at fair              與其公平值變動分開列報。
    value through other comprehensive income (“FVOCI”) are not
    reported separately from other changes in fair value.

    materials, direct labour cost and related production overheads          接 人 工 成 本 及 有 關 生 產 經 常 性 開 支（按
    (based on normal operating capacity). Net realisable value is the       正 常 營 運 能 力 計 算）。可 變 現 淨 值 為 正
    estimated selling price in the ordinary course of business less the     常業務過程中的估計售價扣除估計必需
    estimated costs necessary to make the sale.                             的銷售成本。

 21 TRADE AND BILLS RECEIVABLES AND OTHER                                    21 應收賬款及應收票據及按攤銷成本
    FINANCIAL ASSETS AT AMORTISED COST                                          列賬之其他金融資產

      Material accounting policy                                               重大會計政策
      Trade and bills receivables including retention money receivables        應 收 賬 款 及 應 收 票 據（包 括 應 收 預 扣 金
      are amounts due from customers for merchandise sold or                   額）為 於 正 常 業 務 過 程 中 就 出 售 商 品 或
      services performed in the ordinary course of business. If                提 供 服 務 而 應 收 客 戶 的 款 項。倘 應 收 賬
      collection of trade and bills receivables is expected in one year or     款 及 應 收 票 據 預 期 於 一 年 內（或 如 屬 較
      less (or in the normal operating cycle of the business if longer),       長時間，則於一般業務營運週期）收回，
      they are classified as current assets. If not, they are presented as     則 分 類 為 流 動 資 產。否 則，會 被 呈 列 為
      non-current assets.                                                      非流動資產。

      Trade receivables are recognised initially at the amount of              應收賬款會初始按無條件地可收取的
      consideration that is unconditional, unless they contain                 代 價 金 額 確 認，除 非 其 包 含 重 大 融 資 部
      significant financing components, when they are recognised               分，則 按 其 公 平 值 確 認。本 集 團 持 有 應
      at fair value. The Group holds the trade receivables with the            收 賬 款 之 目 的 為 收 取 合 約 現 金 流 量，因
      objective of collecting the contractual cash flows and therefore         此其後會採用實際利息法按攤銷成本減
      measures them subsequently at amortised cost using the                   去減值撥備計量。
      effective interest method less provision for impairment.

      Receivables are written off when there is no reasonable                  當應收款項没有合理的期望可被回收
      expectation of recovery. Subsequent recoveries of amounts                時，該 等 金 額 將 會 被 撇 銷。其 後 收 回 早
      previously written off are credited against net (impairment              前已撇銷的金額則會與綜合收益表中的
      losses)/reversal of impairment losses on financial assets in the         金融資產之（減值淨虧損）╱減值虧損撥
      consolidated income statement.                                           回淨額沖減。

      The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the expected               本集團按前瞻性基準評估相關債務工具
      credit losses associated with the relevant debt instruments. The         之 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。應 用 之 減 值 方 法 取 決
      impairment methodology applied depends on whether there has              於信貸風險是否已出現大幅增加的情
      been a significant increase in credit risk.                              況。

      For trade receivables, the Group applies the simplified approach         就 應 收 賬 款 而 言，本 集 團 應 用 香 港 財 務
      permitted by HKFRS 9, which requires expected lifetime losses            報 告 準 則 第 9 號 許 可 之 簡 化 方 法，其 規
      to be recognised from initial recognition of the receivables, see        定於初始確認應收款項時予以確認預計
      Note 3.1(b)(ii) for further details.                                     存續期虧損（詳見附註3.1(b)(ii) ）    。

 27 TRADE PAYABLES, OTHER PAYABLES AND                                    27 應付賬款、其他應付賬款及應計款
    ACCRUALS                                                                 項

       Material accounting policy                                           重大會計政策
       Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or services that     應付賬款為於正常業務過程中從供應商
       have been acquired in the ordinary course of business from           取得商品或服務中須承擔的支付義務。
       suppliers.

       Trade and other payables are presented as current liabilities        如 果 付 款 期 限 少 於 一 年（或 如 屬 較 長 時
       if payment is due within one year or less (or in the normal          間，則於一般業務營運週期），應付 賬款
       operating cycle of the business if longer). If not, they are         及 其 他 應 付 賬 款 則 被 呈 列 為 流 動 負 債。
       presented as non-current liabilities.                                否則，會被呈列為非流動負債。

       Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair values     應付賬款及其他應付賬款會初始按公平
       and subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective      值 確 認，其 後 使 用 實 際 利 息 法 按 攤 銷 成
       interest method.                                                     本計量。

 28 DEFERRED INCOME TAX                                                      28 遞延所得稅

      Material accounting policy                                               重大會計政策
      Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability method,     遞延所得稅乃就資產與負債的稅基及其
      on temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets         於綜合財務報表中的賬面值兩者間產生
      and liabilities and their carrying amounts in the consolidated           的 暫 時 性 差 額，並 以 負 債 法 悉 數 進 行 撥
      financial statements. However, deferred tax liabilities are not          備。然 而，倘 遞 延 稅 項 負 債 產 生 自 商 譽
      recognised if they arise from the initial recognition of goodwill.       的 初 始 確 認，則 不 會 被 確 認。倘 遞 延 所
      Deferred income tax is also not accounted for if it arises from          得稅乃產生自於交易（業務合併除外）中
      initial recognition of an asset or liability in a transaction other      對 資 產 或 負 債 的 初 始 確 認，而 於 交 易 當
      than a business combination that at the time of the transaction          時並無影響會計或應課稅溢利或虧損且
      affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss and does not       不產生相等金額的應課稅及可扣減的暫
      give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary differences.         時 性 差 額，亦 不 會 被 確 認。遞 延 所 得 稅
      Deferred income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws)             乃以於報告期末已制定或實質上已制
      that have been enacted or substantially enacted at the end of            定，並 預 期 於 變 現 相 關 遞 延 所 得 稅 資 產
      the reporting period and are expected to apply when the related          或償還相關遞延所得稅負債時適用的稅
      deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred income tax         率（及法例）釐定。
      liability is settled.

      Deferred tax assets are recognised only if it is probable that           遞延稅項資產只會於未來可能有應課稅
      future taxable amounts will be available to utilise those                金額可使用該等暫時性差額及虧損時方
      temporary differences and losses.                                        予以確認。

      Deferred tax liabilities and assets are not recognised for               當海外業務投資的賬面值及稅基產生暫
      temporary differences between the carrying amount and tax                時 性 差 額，而 本 集 團 有 能 力 控 制 暫 時 性
      bases of investments in foreign operations where the Group               差 額 撥 回 的 時 間 點，且 有 可 能 不 會 在 可
      is able to control the timing of the reversal of the temporary           見 將 來 撥 回 時，遞 延 稅 項 負 債 及 資 產 則
      differences and it is probable that the differences will not reverse     不會被確認。
      in the foreseeable future.

      to 2043 (2022: 2023 to 2042). The potential deferred income               課 稅 收 入。所 有 稅 項 虧 損 將 於 二 � 二 四
      tax assets in respect of these tax losses which have not been             年 至 二 � 四 三 年（二 零 二 二 年：二 零 二 三
      recognised will be calculated based on the effective income tax           年 至 二 零 四 二 年）到 期。尚 未 確 認 的 該 等
      rates according to prevailing tax laws and regulations in which the       稅項虧損的潛在遞延所得稅資產將根據
      Group operates.                                                           實際所得稅稅率及當時本集團經營適用

   35.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                  35.1 合併原則及權益會計法
        (i) Subsidiaries                                                        (i) 附屬公司
              Subsidiaries are all entities over which the Group                    附屬公司指本集團對其有控制
              has control. The Group controls an entity when                        權 的 所 有 實 體。本 集 團 對 實 體
              the Group is exposed to, or has rights to, variable                   有 控 制 權，是 指 本 集 團 因 參 與
              returns from its involvement with the entity and has                  該實體的營運而承擔其可變回
              the ability to affect those returns through its power                 報的相關風險或有權享有其可
              to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are              變 回 報，並 能 夠 運 用 其 指 導 實
              fully consolidated from the date on which control is                  體 活 動 的 權 力 影 響 上 述 回 報。
              transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated                     附屬公司在控制權轉移至本集
              from the date that control ceases.                                    團 當 日 全 數 合 併 入 賬。附 屬 公
                                                                                    司在控制權終止之日起停止合
                                                                                    併入賬。

              The acquisition method of accounting is used to                       本集團的業務合併使用收購會
              account for business combinations by the Group (refer                 計法入賬（參考附註35.2 ）
                                                                                                  。
              to Note 35.2).

              Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealised                    集 團 內 公 司 間 交 易、結 餘 及 集
              gains on transactions between group companies are                     團公司間交易的未變現收益均
              eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless              予 以 對 銷。未 變 現 虧 損 亦 會 對
              the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                    銷，除 非 該 交 易 提 供 證 據 顯 示
              of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of                      所 轉 讓 資 產 出 現 減 值 情 況。附
              subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to                     屬公司的會計政策已按需要變
              ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the                   更，以 確 保 與 本 集 團 所 採 納 的
              Group.                                                                政策貫徹一致。

              Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                附屬公司業績及權益的非控股
              subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated                 權 益 分 別 於 綜 合 收 益 表、綜 合
              income statement, consolidated statement of                           全 面 收 益 表、綜 合 權 益 變 動 表
              comprehensive income, consolidated statement of                       及綜合資產負債表內單獨列
              changes in equity and consolidated balance sheet                      示。
              respectively.

       (ii)   Associated companies                                           (ii)   聯營公司
              Associated companies are all entities over which the                  聯營公司指本集團對其有重大
              Group has significant influence but not control or joint              影響力而無控制權或共同控制
              control. This is generally the case where the Group                   權 的 實 體，通 常 情 況 乃 本 集 團
              holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                       持 有20%至50%表 決 權。於 聯
              Investments in associated companies are accounted for                 營公司的投資乃初始按成本確
              using the equity method of accounting (see (iv) below),               認 後，使 用 權 益 會 計 法（見 下
              after initially being recognised at cost.                             文(iv) ）入賬。

      35.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                  35.1 合併原則及權益會計法（續）
          (continued)
          (iii) Joint arrangements                                              (iii) 聯合安排
                Under HKFRS 11 “Joint Arrangements”, investments                   根據香港財務報告準則第11號
                in joint arrangements are classified as either joint               「 聯 合 安 排 」，對 聯 合 安 排 的
                operations or joint ventures. The classification depends           投資須分類為共同經營或合營
                on the contractual rights and obligations of each                  公 司，有 關 分 類 視 乎 各 投 資 者
                investor, rather than the legal structure of the joint             的 合 同 權 益 和 義 務 而 定，而 非
                arrangement. The Group has a joint venture.                        合 營 安 排 的 法 律 結 構。本 集 團
                                                                                   擁有一間合營公司。

                Interests in the joint ventures are accounted for using            於合營公司的權益於綜合資產
                the equity method (see (iv) below), after initially being          負債表按成本初始確認後使用
                recognised at cost in the consolidated balance sheet.              權益法（見下文(iv) ）入賬。


          (iv) Equity method                                                    (iv) 權益會計法
                Under the equity method of accounting, the                         根 據 權 益 會 計 法，投 資 初 始 按
                investments are initially recognised at cost and                   成 本 確 認，隨 後 予 以 調 整 以 確
                adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of              認本集團於被投資方所佔的收
                the post-acquisition profits or losses of the investee in          購後溢利或虧損於損益中及於
                profit or loss, and the Group’s share of movements in              被投資方其他全面收益中所佔
                other comprehensive income of the investee in other                的 其 他 全 面 收 益 變 動。已 收 或
                comprehensive income. Dividend received or receivable              應收聯營公司及合營企業股息
                from associated companies and joint ventures are                   乃確認為投資賬面值的減少。
                recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of
                the investment.

                When the Group’s share of losses in an equity-                     當本集團所佔按權益法入賬之
                accounted investment equals or exceeds its interest in             投資的虧損等於或超過其佔實
                the entity, including any other unsecured long-term                體 的 權 益（包 括 任 何 其 他 無 抵
                receivables, the Group does not recognise further                  押 長 期 應 收 款 項）時，本 集 團
                losses, unless it has incurred obligations or made                 不 再 進 一 步 確 認 虧 損，除 非 本
                payments on behalf of the other entity.                            集團代表其他實體承擔義務或
                                                                                   支付款項。

                Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group                 本集團及其聯營公司間及合營
                and its associated company and joint venture are                   企業間之交易所產生的未變現
                eliminated to the extent of the Group’s interest in                收 益 均 予 以 對 銷（只 限 於 本 集
                these entities. Unrealised losses are also eliminated              團 於 該 等 實 體 的 應 佔 權 益）。
                unless the transaction provides evidence of an                     未 變 現 虧 損 亦 會 對 銷，除 非 該
                impairment of the asset transferred. Accounting                    交易有證據顯示所轉讓資產出
                policies of equity accounted investees have been                   現 減 值 情 況。按 權 益 法 入 賬 之
                changed where necessary to ensure consistency with                 被投資方的會計政策已按需要
                the policies adopted by the Group.                                 變 更，以 確 保 與 本 集 團 所 採 納

      35.2 Business combinations                                             35.2 業務合併
          The acquisition method of accounting is used to account               所有業務合併均以收購會計法入
          for all business combinations, regardless of whether equity           賬，無 論 所 收 購 者 為 權 益 工 具 或 其
          instruments or other assets are acquired. The consideration           他 資 產。收 購 一 間 附 屬 公 司 所 轉 讓
          transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:        的代價包括：

          •    fair values of the assets transferred                            •   所轉讓資產的公平值

          •    liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired        •   對收購對象先前的擁有人所產
               business                                                             生的負債

          •    equity interests issued by the Group                             •   本集團所發行的權益

          •    fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a            •   或然代價安排所產生的任何資
               contingent consideration arrangement, and                            產或負債的公平值；及

          •    fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the            •   於該附屬公司任何先前已存在
               subsidiary.                                                          的權益的公平值

          Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent           除 有 限 例 外 情 況 外，於 業 務 合 併 所
          liabilities assumed in a business combination are, with               收購的可識別資產以及所承擔的可
          limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair values           識 別 負 債 及 或 然 負 債，初 始 按 收 購
          at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-                日 期 的 公 平 值 計 量。本 集 團 根 據 個
          controlling interest in the acquired entity on an acquisition-        別收購交易按公平值或非控股權益
          by-acquisition basis either at fair value or at the non-              應佔被收購實體的可識別資產淨值
          controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired            的比例確認為被收購實體的任何非
          entity’s net identifiable assets.                                     控股權益。

          Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                   收購相關成本於產生時支銷。

          The excess of the:

          •    consideration transferred,                                       •   所轉讓的代價；

          •    amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired           •   於被收購實體的任何非控股權
               entity, and                                                          益金額；及

               interest in the acquired entity                                      於收購日的公平值

          over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is        高於已收購的可識別資產淨值的公
          recorded as goodwill. If those amounts are less than the fair         平值時所產生的差額會按商譽列
          value of the net identifiable assets of the business acquired,        賬。倘 上 述 金 額 低 於 所 收 購 業 務 的
          the difference is recognised directly in the consolidated             可 識 別 資 產 淨 值 的 公 平 值，有 關 差
          income statement as a bargain purchase.                               額會作為一項廉價收購直接於綜合

       statements exceeds the carrying amount in the consolidated              時，會 對 該 等 附 屬 公 司 的 投 資 進 行

       acquisition of the items.                                            本。

       Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying                當與項目有關的未來經濟利益可
       amount or recognised as a separate asset, as appropriate,            能 流 入 本 集 團，以 及 項 目 成 本 能 可
       only when it is probable that future economic benefits               靠 計 算 時，其 後 成 本 才 會 計 入 資 產
       associated with the item will flow to the Group and the              的 賬 面 值 或 確 認 為 獨 立 資 產（如 適
       cost of the item can be measured reliably. The carrying              用）。任何被確認為獨立資產的部分
       amount of any component accounted for as a separate                  的 賬 面 值 會 於 被 替 換 時 終 止 確 認。
       asset is derecognised when replaced. All other repairs               所有其他維修及保養費於其發生的
       and maintenance are charged to the consolidated income               報告期間在綜合收益表中扣除。
       statement during the reporting period in which they are
       incurred.

       An asset’s carrying amount is written down immediately               倘資產的賬面值超過其估計可收回
       to its recoverable amount if the asset’s carrying amount is          金 額，則 其 賬 面 值 將 即 時 撇 減 至 其
       greater than its estimated recoverable amount.                       可收回金額。

       Gains and losses on disposals are determined by comparing            出售資產的收益及虧損指出售所得
       proceeds with carrying amount. These are included in the             款 項 與 賬 面 值 之 間 差 額。該 等 收 益
       consolidated income statements.                                      及虧損已計入綜合收益表。

       Construction in progress represents property, plant and              在 建 工 程 指 在 建 或 待 安 裝 的 物 業、
       equipment under construction or pending installation, and is         廠 房 及 設 備，並 按 成 本 扣 除 減 值 虧
       stated at cost less impairment losses. Cost comprises direct         損 列 賬。 成 本 為 施 工 期 間 直 接 建
       costs of construction including borrowing costs attributable         造 成 本（包 括 與 工 程 相 關 的 借 貸 成
       to the construction during the period of construction.               本）。

      35.6 Impairment of non-financial assets                                35.6 非金融資產的減值
          Goodwill is not subject to amortisation and is tested annually       商譽不會被攤銷並需要每年為其進
          for impairment, or more frequently if events or changes in           行 減 值 測 試，或 倘 有 事 件 或 情 況 變
          circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other            動 表 明 其 可 能 出 現 減 值，則 會 更 頻
          assets are tested for impairment whenever events or changes          繁 地 進 行 減 值 測 試。其 他 資 產 須 於
          in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may not           指出其未必可收回賬面值的事件或
          be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for the             變 動 發 生 時 測 試 減 值。減 值 虧 損 為
          amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its              資產賬面值高於其可收回價值的金
          recoverable amount. The recoverable amount is the higher             額。可 收 回 金 額 為 資 產 的 公 平 值 減
          of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal and value in         處 置 成 本 後 的 價 值，與 其 使 用 價 值
          use. For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets are            兩 者 中 的 較 高 者。為 了 評 估 資 產 減
          grouped at the lowest levels for which there are separately          值，本 集 團 按 可 獨 立 地 確 認 其 現 金
          identifiable cash inflows which are largely independent of           流入且其現金流入很大程度上獨立
          the cash inflows from other assets or groups of assets (cash-        於其他資產或資產組合的最低層次
          generating units). Non-financial assets other than goodwill          單 位（現 金 產 生 單 位）將 資 產 分 類。
          that suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible                除 商 譽 外，已 蒙 受 減 值 的 非 金 融 資
          reversal of the impairment at the end of each reporting              產在各報告期末均會就減值是否可
          period.                                                              以撥回進行檢討。

      35.8 Offsetting financial instruments                                   35.8 金融工具抵銷
           Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount         倘本集團當前有合法且可強制執行
           is reported in the consolidated balance sheet where the                的權利抵銷所確認金額及擬按淨額
           Group currently has a legally enforceable right to offset              基準結算或變現資產及同時償還負
           the recognised amounts, and there is an intention to settle            債 時，則 該 等 金 融 資 產 與 負 債 可 相
           on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability           互 抵 銷，並 以 淨 額 於 綜 合 資 產 負 債
           simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not be              表 內 呈 報。該 法 定 可 執 行 權 利 必 須
           contingent on future events and must be enforceable in                 不 得 依 據 未 來 事 件 而 定，而 在 一 般
           the normal course of business and in the event of default,             業務過程中以及倘本集團或對手方
           insolvency or bankruptcy of the Group or the counterparty.             一 旦 出 現 違 約、無 償 債 能 力 或 破 產
                                                                                  時，依然具有約束力。

                prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.

   35.15 Provisions                                                                     35.15 撥備
        Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                              當本集團因過往事件承擔現有法
        legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events,                        律 或 推 定 性 責 任，而 履 行 責 任 有 可
        it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required                        能 導 致 資 源 的 流 出，且 金 額 能 夠 可
        to settle the obligation and the amount has been reliably                           靠 地 估 算 的 情 況 下，撥 備 便 會 被 確
        estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future operating                       認。未 來 經 營 虧 損 不 會 被 確 認 為 撥
        losses.                                                                             備。

        Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                                倘 承 擔 若 干 類 似 責 任，於 釐 定 履 行
        likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement                           責 任 導 致 資 源 流 出 的 可 能 性 時，將
        is determined by considering the class of obligations as a                          以整類責任類別作為整體予以考
        whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an                       慮。即 使 同 類 責 任 當 中 任 何 一 項 導
        outflow with respect to any one item included in the same                           致 資 源 流 出 的 可 能 性 甚 低，撥 備 亦
        class of obligations may be small.                                                  會被確認。

        P ro v i s i o n s a re m e a s u re d a t t h e p re s e n t v a l u e o f         撥備以管理層於報告期末為履行現
        management’s best estimate of the expenditures required                             有責任所需支出的最佳估算現值計
        to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting                        量。計 算 此 等 現 值 使 用 的 貼 現 率 為
        period. The discount rate used to determine the present                             能夠反映當前市場的貨幣時間價值
        value is a pre-tax rate that reflects current market                                及 該 負 債 特 有 風 險 的 稅 前 貼 現 率。
        assessments of the time value of money and the risks                                時 間 流 逝 導 致 撥 備 金 額 的 增 幅，會
        specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to                     被確認為利息開支。
        the passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

      35.17 Leases (as lessee)                                                       35.17 租賃（作為承租人）
           Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                          於租賃資產可供本集團使用的日
           corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset                期，本 集 團 會 就 租 賃 確 認 使 用 權 資
           is available for use by the Group.                                           產及相應負債。

           Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                               合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組
           components. The Group allocates the consideration in the                     成部分。本集團根據相對獨立價格將
           contract to the lease and non-lease components based on                      合同中的對價分配為租賃和非租賃
           their relative stand-alone prices.                                           組成部分。

           Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially                    租賃產生的資產及負債初始以現值
           measured on a present value basis.                                           進行計量。

           Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance                   租賃付款會於本金及財務費用之間
           cost. The finance cost is charged to the consolidated income                 作 出 分 配。財 務 費 用 會 在 租 賃 期 間
           statement over the lease period so as to produce a constant                  於 綜 合 收 益 表 扣 除，藉 以 令 各 期 間
           periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of the                    的負債餘額的週期利率保持一致。
           liability for each period.

           Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the                      使 用 權 資 產 按 成 本 計 量，包 括 以 下
           following:                                                                   各項：

           •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability,               •   租賃負債的初始計量金額；

           •    a n y l e a s e p a y m e n t s m a d e a t o r b e f o re t h e        •   於開始日期或之前作出的任何
                commencement date less any lease incentives received,                       租賃付款減去任何已收取租賃
                and                                                                         優惠；及

           •    any initial direct costs.                                               •   任何初始直接成本。

           Payments associated with short-term leases of premises                       與短期物業租賃及所有低價值資產
           and all leases of low-valued assets are recognised on a                      之租賃相關的付款會以直線法於綜
           straight-line basis as an expense in the consolidated income                 合 收 益 表 確 認 為 開 支。短 期 租 賃 為
           statement. Short-term leases are leases with a lease term of                 租 賃 期12個 月 或 以 下 並 沒 有 購 買 選
           12 months or less without a purchase option.                                 擇權的租賃。
